/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ K TOI SANG A :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ YEU DEN THE A /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ HANG LE CHO TAO /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ DAY DAY BAT DAU /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ YEU SINH LY A /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ MAY SUA DI NE /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ CON CHO CAY A /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ SAO CAY TAO DO? /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ MAY SUA DI NE /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ SAO CAY TAO HA? /꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ O O LAI WIN  ⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰ :⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰/꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰⃟꙰꙰⃟꙰
